People are weird writ large or individually. Martin was a large individual. This next one is large in a different way.


This one is about someone -Hi for my alternative self- who's biggest problem is a gut he's had since seventh grade. Maybe it looks like a beer belly so 'that guy might be fun. Many parties I bet' or 'no beer stains on him. A steady grip. Hehe' or WORSE for the story, 'A Dad bod! I could make him a dad again.'


"Oh look at the author glazing himself. 'I'm not fat, I have a dad bod.'" No I'm fat, but not as huge as I could be if that makes sense. And I know WHY.


In seventh grade I wanted money for things. I only had money for lunch, and no allowance. So I pocketed my lunch money. I didn't touch breakfast at the time -I still don't really. So I'd get home ravenous. And maybe with a new stupid toy I walked to a department store to buy.


My stomach started digesting itself. I think my mother thought I was also buying caloric garbage as I started pudging up. I did do some stress eating. She did as well so it seemed to add up in her mind. She parted proverbial oceans to get me on the school lunch program.


However, entry into the lunch program came a bit late to undo the damage. We didn't have a whole lot so other things lead to more… gas-in-the-paretenium? Feast and famine took their toll. And I never recovered.


Recently I got over hereditary nonsense for a good 6-13 month stretch, and even then I still looked like I was snacking. No one could have guessed I was living on nothing. Proverbially flying on fumes. "Fumes?" $10 container of pretzels… you could SMELL THEM on my clothes and pores. Fumes two different ways. I had one rod a day.


When I did have money again, I got back to healthier eating habits. The gut just looked like it never left. So it looked then as it does now: like I'm eating well.


So maybe this alternative fictional me is financially solvent. And other leaps of logic, and illogic.


As you might guess, IRL this means nada. It's 2026 at the time of this… -is this a preface now… hmm… yeah- and unless you look to be a millionaire, give up.

------And Lo It Came To Pass----
                       that 'he' said the last words he would ever say as a _Free_Citizen_ of anywhere. He spoke them to his wife and coworker, a one Elliott Smith PhD (Physics), while standing at the center of three standing desks covered in several dozen monitors of just as many makes and models.


Dr. Elliott Smith was behind thick borosilicate glass made into a barricade with old school Cold War grade chemical tech: bakelite :to keep any and all reactive interference at bay.


The alien threat had been known to 'play' with magnetic fields subtly, or at least the one encountered entity had been known to with some difficulty while claiming that Others had more 'juice' to work with. A threat deemed IMPERMISSABLE by the man standing in accusation of wife and the sky.


"I know better!" proclaimed a man in his late forties, with no hair left on his head, and shaved off of his face poorly, from beyond the sturdy barricade. He pointed an arthritic, accusatory finger first at his detractor, and then at the sky -or tiled ceiling as it were.


"God damnit man give the scientists some time to figure this out! It's just one crackhead monster!" Elliott pleaded. Continuing softer into the intercom, "We've been over this! Hon, Stand down. This can't be undone," he pointed at the key in front of his husband like it was something venomous. "It's just one monster," he repeated more at the glass than his husband. Behind him, blocked from view, military contract workers were trying desperately to pry open the sealed door. For now, at this doomed hour, it was a private dialogue.


And Elliott's body language gave every impression he'd lost, as he sat on the ground cradling both his access card and the forged access card he always kept with him. In crayon it read simply,"Verney-poo," his nickname for his soon to be courts and everything else marshalled husband, Vernier Smith PhD (Astrophysics)(Quantum Mechanics), and it might be the only physical sign
anyone loved the man. Soon it would be the only name for him not actively cursed by laymen and scholars alike.


"For now!" the scene continued "And I don't care what God Damns. In fact:


I AM BETTER THAN GOD!


I AM A SAVIOUR.


I AM NOT SOME MOLDY FORCE SITTING ON A THRONE CUTE AS YOU PLEASE IN AN IVORY TOWER NOT GIVING A SHIT ABOUT _HIS_ CREATIONS. I AM A SAVIOUR! LIKE _HIS_ SON" he slammed a fist on the central podium before his tall frame.


"BUT BETTER!" Vernier turned towards the figure of Elliott beyond the barricade, and looked down at where he now sat, "FOR I WILL SAVE EVERYONE! YOU WILL SEE my love. EVERYONE WILL!," he smiled a smile brimming with zeal. He grabbed the key firmly, readouts lit up, and other lights hummed into
existence in sequence. Many of those lights not even in the room with him.


All screens indicated readiness.


The years of doubt, the last several weeks of ridicule, melted off him as he straightened to his full and stout height and intoned,


"BEAR WITNESS TO SALVATION!!"


as he turned the key which set off the chain reaction of all the other keys being turned -for none could be trusted to turn them in sync, nor at Dr. Smith's whim- and the bombs lit up on the viewing monitors like holiday lights in a myriad of colors that all shifted to a blinding searing light before the remote observing cameras died in this great strike against...


Not threats to peace, honor, justice, civility... nothing so noble.


It was a strike against HIS ego. That alien, now vaporized at ground zero in his subdimension, had always pointed all his comments, quips, and other derision directly at Vernier. The 'Thrustmaster' was seemingly the only one ever targeted so. You could even argue that as Vernier was the only one
riled up, he may literally have been the only one targeted at that.


A Chosen Target to manipulate into -for some odd reason- doing exactly what was just done under The Eyes of God.


And what was done? What exactly transpired? Let the record show that:


A Single 'Alien' entity stating the obvious lie that they were a scout for
        an invasion of BIBLICAL magnitude. A Force so vast as to push humanity
        off of the pages of history as not even a footnote.
An Astrophysicist, Dr. Vernier Smith, who was only called in to consult because
        he could put aside the PhD and speak to laymen, generals, or even clergy just
        as fluently as he might craft his next thesis
A Physicist, Dr. Elliott Smith, part of a Governmental task force and a liaison to
        a S.E.T.I. adjacent program seeking Peace amongst the stars (until Earth
        could hold its own in a fight at least)


... might as well have been the only sentient parties to this Catastrophe.


World Leaders, heads of state, parliamentary bodies, and military minds while supporting an initial push to have a show of force ready, agreed to not ACTUALLY arm anything until the scientists said so. Logistical frameworks did most of the rest of the prepwork SHOULD anything be deemed Go Time.


Go Time was never set, and likely at least a year out, even if the invasion was tomorrow. Go Time was seen as Never Time. Again. The Threat was


OBVIOUS TO EVERYONE TO BE A LIE.


The Scientists, theoretical, quantum-mechanical, quantum-physical or otherwise, only agreed that under no circumstances anything BE done so soon after discovery. There was no consensus to STOP anything from being ready, and so the technical frameworks did most of the rest of the prepwork SHOULD anything be deemed Go Time.


That's Two Frameworks chugging away.


Spiritual Leaders almost unanimously found the Alien a charlatan, but as they had no real say except admonishment, various other frameworks did most of the rest of the prepwork SHOULD anything be deemed Go Time.


And with that three PLUS frameworks churned their task expertly. You would think that maybe pure bureaucratic bullshit would gum up the works. Fear removes bureaucracy, and Verny-poo shares his fear with everyone he entreats. One guy can't change minds.


Anyone would think that. Dr. Smith however was his own cult of personality. He knew what names to bring to bear, what hints to drop, and even what lies to use FROM THE ENEMY COMBATANT'S OWN MOUTHS. Yes. Plural.


Vernier could move MOUNTAINS.




Now he was rewriting them.


In one act of pure, weaponized, unshackled ego, HE decided he knew better. HE DECIDED that this one situation was HIS PERSONAL 'Let us use Cane Toads anyway' moment, where he was absolutely justified.


Damn the consequences. No consequence could possibly matter.


In one act, just turning a key, such a simple thing, everything Humanity thought wss set in stone... wasn't.

He got that.


Vernier pushed for, and achieved, a massive 'Superman 4, The Quest for Peace' level of ordinance. He fanned the flames under everyone's collective asses to keep them motivated. He had the ordinance primed. Anything non-nuclear was replaced with nuclear following concerns that wouldn't stand up under scrutiny which there wasn't time for. "What if..." it's said he started to everyone in the final stretch,"we're down to the wire, people are being slaughtered via liminal weapons systems and… only one person makes it to their post."
His performance was said in hindsight even to be Oscar worthy, his lines were mere middle school FOMO,"One person cannot activate this system. We're all dead and invaded." Passing his fear like a baton, he had continued,"Do you want to be the death of humanity? Do you want to meet that poor sod who was READY at the Pearly Gates to say 'Sorry we're all dead. I'm sorry I obeyed the wrong orders.' Do you want that?"


As if they were puppeted by his zeal or programmed with him as their personal author, barricades fell and systems linked.


Down to one key, systemically linked to dozens of other keys that if THAT one COULD JUST TURN, all of them would. A Domino effect in-place of warm bodies.


Make no mistake. There was no single 'Dr. Mudd', outside of the Catastrophic Dr. Vernier, there were slews of the convinced, big swaths of people who could say,'My name is Mudd.' Swaths of people reassured time and again on the sanity of the project itself, and the heads of those involved. Swaths convinced time was absolutely of the essence. All on the conviction of one Zealot in a Labcoat that seemed as sane as you or anyone else. He was just a guy trying to save everyone. He had the time to face down every reservation… 


FOR THE GOOD OF HUMANITY.


Every dissention raised was calmly rationally put to bed with a glass of something warm and a bed time story. If Dr. Elliott Smith, was not a very light sleeper, there might not have EVEN BEEN an attempt to stop


The Liminal Catastrophe.


All this seemed to ebb and flow through reality itself as time stilled back to normal from the detonations. The door was pried open, one shot from something collapsed the barrier, and snapped Ellliot Smith, PhD from his misery euphoria to snap his eyes to the opening and the rifle aiming at his husband the Catastrophic Doctor. He bolted for Verny-poo to save him for the trial destined for him in the aftermath of


That Damned Key Turn.

EVERYONE. EVERYWHERE. WILL STANDDOWN UNTIL THE CONSEQUENSES HAVE BEEN MET OUT.


Not one soul was asleep. The words, as pure CONCEPTUAL MEANING, knocked everyone asleep awake, and any distracted no longer were. A Word like that can't be sidelined for what looks like magic, or mundane LEDs doing their job.


It simply cannot.

The Timeline is backwards when it is in sequence at all. It's on purpose. We start 'now' jump to forever ago like months after the event -emotionally anyway- finish that bit, and then jump back FURTHER, then forward a bit again, before jumping to the END of this chapter's storyline with the alluded to court case. Something like 1 3 -1 2 4 shenanigans. That’s not exact either.


In complete timeline order might not work for the piece. Feel free to disagree.


Like I almost broke out my dice bag for any time a pronoun came up EVEN IF I ALREADY ESTABLISHED THEM just to have some whimsy. I can easily go this dark. But the unrelenting dark peppered with dalliances with silly isn’t enough whimsy.


Not that this is Now and Then, Here and There. /BUT IT IS CLOSE./ Liminal Earth is not a Shining Utopia. Liminal Earth is not a Crapsack World. It just is. And now that I know ‘This Earth’ needed to be a Liminal Earth I have to put into place all the fallout from such a decision.


Also the elements of The Arc that need to ooze (positive) into place need a point they started properly happening. Surely Joy was BUSY. Yes. Boy howdy, yes. Surely Hope was busy. Also Yes. Did it take a week to figure out what was suddenly happening? Very also yes.


‘How can there be a timeline, a history rife with combat, for a situation that had not yet happened? Hope and Joy were in the PAST of this Liminal Shit.’


The turn of that key set everything in motion that needed to have ‘Always Been There’. Part of why if Very-poo wasn’t Elsewhere, there’d be a meteor aimed at his skull… and that might lead to other deaths.


Logic Breaking creation at its lowest and finest. Lamarckian Writing. Because I want ‘A’ logic. Nothing in writing is perfect. This logic is far from it.


‘Then why explain it!’


Because * I * have to. You don’t have to like it or read it. The lack of a why was killing me.

     The Death Situation on Liminal Earth WAS wild. Now these days, outside Eld’s door,
Death does their job. Dying happens, but in those first 'days' Death was held back. Only certain sanctioned deaths happened. Open and shut with obvious Sinful Sorts kinda sanctioned. ‘The Father hates mass murderers,’ kinda schtick. Most just got sentenced and waited for The Death Situation to resolve itself. Some were put into comas, as that was not death, but kept crimes even towards other inmates on pause. Most just got to twiddle their thumbs like nothing was different.


It took some effort to get a sanctioned death.


So... any high profile cases that survived their court dates? You need to think on a much more granular scale. Shivs in prison. Blunted. Firing Squads. Shooting Bubbles. Hangings in Hidden corners? Barely even choking, and the perpetrators likely folded in half in pain for trying as the ropes snap. Some of the 'no one dies' actively were being enforced by divinely Mandated Black ops teams. With Angels, demons, devils, and holy warriors for the bigger cessations of death. Newer elements were often hard to coordinate with. Sneering imperialism abounded. Attitudes and tactics were often incompatible, even if that was only in perception alone.


Subduing tactics? Fine. Drone on Drone? Fine. Tasering them unconscious and freezing time so it just didn’t wear off, and the perp(s) could be carried around like baggage? Yikes no. A ‘spank ray’ that made them itch furiously and kept them from most actions? Also yikes to many of these contractors. So to many of these Divine Enforcers, anything outside their native purview was just not even looked into.


IF their purview even allowed it. Often not so much as a squad leader came along. Billions of snap decisions often saved lives at a one to one level.


Remember when the courts were first brought up. . .? Well for starters everyone made it to their court date. No extra attorneys from on high but they made those dates. Some of these court appearances were now considered mandatory and ENFORCED. Why? It is one thing to dodge a subpoena over a parking ticket. Bigger Sins tended to get enforcement. Bail Jumping is serious. Bail Jumping for cases of Greed slightly less weighty than Murders from before the Catastrophe.


"Oh God in heaven, please let Barbara Murderstrike make it to court so they are imprisoned forever. She keeps hiding in foreign lands, and might make it out of the CE at this rate. Please," Someone might have prayed. These kinds were likely heard and mobilized over. If you Pray for Guidance you get Guidance. You pray for Investigation? Oh you get that investigation alright.


Often a prayer pinpointed problems to keep things mostly smooth. The implementation of a Scalpel vs a Sword Vs a Truncheon can be quite telling after the fact.




No quantity of prayer can really help. Several prayers by several individuals can grease the solution to a speedier resolution. Then the squad can hit the next problem running.


Make no mistake: quality moves mountains. A good quality prayer really sticks out


"Lord. I know you are listening. Which is a thing now. I’m so very glad of it. Watching over everyone from on high but really feeling close by is amazing. And the Guardian Angels catching a knife here or a bullet there from hurting someone is just incredible,” he adjusted his position in his cell. He listened for anyone coming to interrupt. There’s no talking to yourself in solitary. Especially when you requested it. Madmen went elsewhere.


Of course madmen didn’t tell their stories and get listened to. Well maybe if they prayed.

     Barbara turned from the window. That giant locusts were eating everything at her industrial farm was unnerving to watch. They were planting new crops per the orders of that dude with the robe and the book in their wake. It was unnerving seeing the new crops shoot up in seconds.


She didn't know she had GMO anything to warrant this. She didn't know her farm was being re-optimized away from modern industrial farming for some magically enhanced crap she wanted nothing to do with. But the GMO crops were crying or some shit. Grow, die, get fed to people. Barbara didn't CARE.


But apparently on her end nothing would change. Well yields were gonna soar. Buying more trucks would be a headache. Could she rig harness on the giant rolly pollies that were her new harvesters? Or if she was really angrily whimsical... is she getting a flying sleigh and reindeer?!


The grapevine said this was happening all over. Her life was being forcingly switched from one thing she understood and had the skills for to another thing she mostly didn't understand, but her skills perfectly transferred over for. Which she didn't believe. Even if she'd demonstrated it already. It'd break the second Harry Potter with a ?Kindle? left.


This was college all over again. And in college she buried herself in social media.


She fired up her tablet, waited till the TV bothered to let the tablet connect, and got ready to scroll.


"That shit out there is awful. Show me who I can blame," her tablet asked for more details,"Right. 'Um user? data so I can give context' There are wizards dancing giant locusts in my field eating all 'unnatural' crops."


"Here's what I could find on that. Oh Note. That wizard is a ..."


"I don't care."


"Fair enough (^_^) your context on who to blame."


There was a video of some Aggri Pundit, "Yes, your crops are being turfed out. Yes, your soil is being enhanced back to a higher standard of richness. But no this is not some divine or governmental mandate. We don't know yet who is behind this... science and magic miracle... What else do I call it. Everything you do NOW translates over. Some of that makes sense. Some of it is bizarre."


"Next article."


"On the current subject?"


"Yeah," she was getting fed up. Maybe she needed to tweak the settings again... but at least it wasn't talking about some celebrity's high tech makeover that let them get back into acting.


"Most of the other articles on the subject are rehashing this story."


Barabara screamed in frustration,"Show me all the headlines!"


She scrolled and scrolled and scrolled. Nothing. She put the tablet back to sleep exasperated. "I don't care what the rich fucks are doing to cope. And save a few NONE of them are coping! That does not distract ME from how I'M NOT COPING!! Don't tell me this shit!" She'd already turned the tablet back on mid-rant. She couldn't escape her need to be online.


The news pages had refreshed with a fresh article,"OH my days! Articles on how woe is me the poor are don't help anyone either. Fuck them. They can't buy better bread. Boo fucking hoo. The Flying feather duster are keeping you alive aren't they?!?" She calmed a sec,"At least they aren't robbing me to feed the welfare shits. My fields. My grain. My money. Period. I need a distraction. A real one," the house shook,"Fuck you damn bugs." Barbara steadied her milk in the bridge and got out her butter to warm it. Then another rumble and she told the ceiling,"Move quieter." She measured exact amounts for her watchtime snack. There was precisesly on bowl before she was out, "Reality is out to fuck me. No dinner no sweet talk. Okay."


She started her popcorn, and got ready to at least WATCH something instead of the... giant nasty beasts eating crops SHE PAID GOOD MONEY FOR. And no she didn't care that she was getting value back. Her decision was stolen from her by god damned Harry Potter!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA!

Eld: "what are cruiseliner speeds in open water on straightaways."
"In open water on straightaways, most cruise ships typically travel at 18 to 22 knots (about 21 to 25 mph or 33 to 41 km/h), balancing fuel efficiency, schedule timing, and passenger comfort.


Typical vs. Top Speeds
• Standard cruising speed: Most modern cruise ships cruise around 18–22 knots, with mega-ships often sitting near 18–20 knots because larger hulls face more resistance and fuel costs rise sharply at higher speeds.
• Maximum speed: Many cruise vessels can reach roughly 22 to 30 knots (up to ~35 mph), with dedicated ocean liners like Queen Mary 2 capable of around 30 knots. Ships rarely run at full throttle during normal sailings due to fuel burn, emissions rules, and vibration. For more details on why ships hold back from top speed, see Emma Cruises."
The average speed is higher but I'm flattening the curve to 18 knots.

"At 18 knots, a mega cruise ship covers 432 nautical miles (about 497 statute miles or 800 km) every 24 hours of continuous sailing, with a typical maximum range of around 3,000 to 5,000+ nautical miles before needing to refuel depending on tank capacity and burn rate."

He needed something quiet for his talents. At least that’s what he was basically told to his face by his CO.


To this day he doesn’t know HOW strings were pulled to get him a real job with a discharge on his military record. And not into a looney bin AS a patient. However there’s a problem. It’s a job he does not want to lose. Threats to fire him cause him to tuck his tail between his legs and beg to not be fired. “You wanna be thrown back in the rat race? To the starving wolves?!”


“N-no! No wolves. Anything!”


“Then you’re gonna take this parcel of cash to wipe away those tears and do as I say.”


“Yes sir.”


“And if the warden asks?”


“The warden will hear nothing from me. I’d rather die.”


“Good boy.”

AI Overview [[Yes I use them from time to time. There's a trigger warning for this.]]
*snip*
1. Kenophobia (Fear of Empty Spaces)
* Definition: Kenophobia is a specific phobia involving an intense, irrational fear or anxiety triggered by empty spaces, voids, vast open areas, or sparsely furnished environments. [1, 2]
* Experience: In an empty city, the lack of human presence turns familiar boundaries and protective crowds into a vast, exposed void, which can trigger physical symptoms like dizziness, rapid heart rate, or panic. [1]
2. Kenopsia (The Eerie Void of Deserted Places)
* Definition: Kenopsia is a term (popularized in modern lexicons like The Dictionary of Obscure Sorrows) describing the uncanny, melancholic atmosphere of a place that is usually bustling with people—like a shopping mall, school hallway, or city street—but is now completely quiet and abandoned. [1]
* Experience: It is not always active terror, but rather the haunting feeling of past life lingering in a space stripped of its purpose ("the ghost of human activity"). [1]
3. Agoraphobia
* Definition: Agoraphobia is a clinical anxiety disorder where a person fears situations where escape might be difficult or help unavailable—often tied to open spaces, public areas, or crowds, though it can extend to feeling unsafe outside the home or in unfamiliar environments. [1, 2]
* Context: Navigating large, deserted, or unfamiliar urban grids can activate this survival response because the absence of people removes a sense of safety or social scaffolding.
4. Ghost Cities and Real-World Examples
* Physical Reality: Entire planned developments or urban districts—such as large-scale housing projects in parts of China or abandoned industrial towns like Chernobyl—stand as literal empty cities with modern skyscrapers, highways, and storefronts completely devoid of life. [1]
* Psychological Reaction: Seeing architecture built explicitly for thousands of people sitting in total silence subverts human evolutionary expectations, as our brains rely on ambient social cues to signal that an environment is safe.
